It embeds a clone of the source graph (as a sub-\/asset), the C\# source of any custom (non-\/package) nodes used by the graph, the source of the shaders referenced by those nodes, and the source of any property drawers referenced by shaders in the graph that are not part of the \doxylink{namespace_genesis}{Genesis} package. Shaders and drawers are stored as source text because runnable Shader/\+\+Drawer objects cannot live as sub-\/assets; the receiver reimports them }{\pageref{class_ahah_games_1_1_genesis_noise_1_1_graph_1_1_genesis_noise_package}}{}

\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_exceptions_1_1_voronoi_doesnt_have_sites_exception}{Sharp\+Voronoi\+Lib.\+Exceptions.\+Voronoi\+Doesnt\+Have\+Sites\+Exception}} }{\pageref{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_exceptions_1_1_voronoi_doesnt_have_sites_exception}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_edge}{Sharp\+Voronoi\+Lib.\+Voronoi\+Edge}} \\*The line segment making the Voronoi cells, i.\+e. the points equidistant to the two nearest Voronoi sites. These are the lines in the Voronoi\+Site.\+Cell. This has Voronoi\+Point end points, i.\+e. Start and End. This has the two Voronoi\+Sites they separate, i.\+e. Right and Left. This connects in a Neighbours node graph to other Voronoi\+Edges, i.\+e. shares end points with them }{\pageref{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_edge}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_ahah_games_1_1_genesis_noise_1_1_nodes_1_1_voronoi_from_points_compute_node}{Ahah\+Games.\+Genesis\+Noise.\+Nodes.\+Voronoi\+From\+Points\+Compute\+Node}} }{\pageref{class_ahah_games_1_1_genesis_noise_1_1_nodes_1_1_voronoi_from_points_compute_node}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_ahah_games_1_1_genesis_noise_1_1_nodes_1_1_voronoi_noise_node}{Ahah\+Games.\+Genesis\+Noise.\+Nodes.\+Voronoi\+Noise\+Node}} }{\pageref{class_ahah_games_1_1_genesis_noise_1_1_nodes_1_1_voronoi_noise_node}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_exceptions_1_1_voronoi_not_tessellated_exception}{Sharp\+Voronoi\+Lib.\+Exceptions.\+Voronoi\+Not\+Tessellated\+Exception}} }{\pageref{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_exceptions_1_1_voronoi_not_tessellated_exception}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_plane}{Sharp\+Voronoi\+Lib.\+Voronoi\+Plane}} \\*An Euclidean plane where a Voronoi diagram can be constructed from Voronoi\+Sites producing a tesselation of cells with Voronoi\+Edge line segments and Voronoi\+Point vertices }{\pageref{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_plane}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_point}{Sharp\+Voronoi\+Lib.\+Voronoi\+Point}} \\*The vertices/\+nodes of the Voronoi cells, i.\+e. the points equidistant to three or more Voronoi sites. These are the end points of a Voronoi\+Edge. These are the Voronoi\+Site.\+Points. Also used for some other derived locations }{\pageref{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_point}}{}
\item\contentsline{section}{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_site}{Sharp\+Voronoi\+Lib.\+Voronoi\+Site}} \\*The point/\+site/\+seed on the Voronoi plane. This has a Cell of Voronoi\+Edges. This has Points of Voronoi\+Points that are the edge end points, i.\+e. the cell\textquotesingle{}s vertices. This also has Neighbours, i.\+e. Voronoi\+Sites across the Voronoi\+Edges }{\pageref{class_sharp_voronoi_lib_1_1_voronoi_site}}{}
